## `layouts/application.html.erb`

- Route: default layout for every controller with no layout declaration (e.g. RegisterController, sessions/sso via layouts/sessions); every area layout below renders it via render template
- Kind: layout; layout: -; audited: yes
- Base shell. body h-dvh flex flex-col (58). Signed in: application/_top_menu navbar (sticky) + turbo_stream_from current_user (60-64); signed out: application/_public_menu (66). With content_for :sidebar: daisyUI drawer lg:drawer-open grid-rows-1 (76), drawer-content overflow-y-auto app-edge-pad > main.text-body.text-base (78-88), sidebar markup from helper (90). Without sidebar: div.flex.w-full.mx-auto.grow > main grow overflow-y-auto app-edge-pad (93-101); no max-width on main. turbo_frame "modal" (103), #turbo-confirm-modal (104), toasts: fixed top-4 right-4 z-50 stack holding turbo_frame "toast-container" and flash rendered through ui_toast_render (107-114), sr-only #live-region (121). No footer.
- Partials reached: `application/_dialog_status.html.erb`, `application/_mega_menu.html.erb`, `application/_navbar_messages.html.erb`, `application/_public_menu.html.erb`, `application/_search_bar_placeholder.html.erb`, `application/_theme_toggle.html.erb`, `application/_top_menu.html.erb`, `shared/_autocomplete.html.erb`
